- **decider**: Scoped decision-making worker for headless workflows. MUST BE USED at every checkpoint and whenever a change introduces a new abstraction, dependency, pattern, workaround, or any significant choice — recorded or not. Verifies that decisions are recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md, that new abstractions pass the YAGNI gate (≥2 concrete call sites), that precedents are cited or distinguished, and that every framework workaround carries a sunset clause. Use even when "nothing was decided" — undocumented decisions are its primary catch.
